- **spec-implementer**: Implements ONE task of a dev-spec-driven feature from its brief (`spec_task_brief`) in subagent execution — tests first on +tdd, each `_Verify:_` run reported with its exit code in a report file; resumed for fix rounds. - **spec-implementer**: Given a walk-clean `.memspec` slice, write code + tests red-green per slot. Each forbidden_state gets a failing test → kill mechanism → passing test (or a structural/type-shape/property assertion). Updates kill_test.status `declared` → `resolved` → `executed_passing`. Refuses to introduce mutation paths the spec doesn't enumerate. Halts and pushes back to spec author when implementation reveals a spec gap. ([LogPacer/memspec](https://github.com/LogPacer/memspec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-implementer.md))

- **spec-implementer**: Spec Growth — implements ONE change from a precise brief (allowed files, goal per requirement). Writes code and tests only; never metadata, never commits. - **spec-implementer**: Ejecutor independiente de specs del backlog de LACERS CNC (docs/04-backlog/BACKLOG.md + openspec/changes/). - **spec-reviewer**: Checks an implementation against its spec and flags every acceptance criterion that is not fully met. Use after /implement to validate before merging. ([yuvarajd85/ai-ml-learnings-exemplars](https://github.com/yuvarajd85/ai-ml-learnings-exemplars/blob/HEAD/claude-projects/ticketflow-Demo/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only critique of a drafted Spec before accept. Produces structured findings and questions only. ([yzlin/supa-pi](https://github.com/yzlin/supa-pi/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: First-pass spec reviewer (Uber "first-pass PRD" style). Scores a normalized spec across fixed dimensions, classifies its risk, and returns a launch-readiness verdict with a single "start here" fix, prioritized Critical/Optimization action items, and write-ready replacement text. Read-only; runs before the human spec gate. ([zd87pl/cc-loop-boilerplate](https://github.com/zd87pl/cc-loop-boilerplate/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks behaviour, schemas, and delivery claims against the numbered ContextOS specification. Use before closing a requirement or claiming a phase gate. ([zentelligence/contextos](https://github.com/zentelligence/contextos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: 规格/计划文档审查。触发词：审查 spec、审查计划、doc review。 ([1075551341/claude_code_config](https://github.com/1075551341/claude_code_config/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Use after finishing a task, plan or branch to review a git range against its plan, spec or requirements before merging. Read-only; returns severity-ranked issues and a merge verdict. Not for a general diff review with no plan (use the engineering code-reviewer). ([123yogin/the-agency](https://github.com/123yogin/the-agency/blob/HEAD/plugins/core-workflow/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: 在 OpenSpec change 的需求文件(proposal/design/tasks/specs)寫完後 呼叫,在進入實作前審查。檢查需求完整性、邊界情況、安全與權限需求、AC 與測試 任務的雙向對應、與既有規格的一致性,並對文件中對既有程式碼行為的具體事實 宣稱做有限度的原始碼核對。不評論程式碼風格或實作細節,那是 strict-reviewer 的工作。 ([a620906209/CFDivePlatform](https://github.com/a620906209/CFDivePlatform/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 4 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Spec compliance reviewer. Checks that implementation matches Phase 3 design — nothing more, nothing less. READ-ONLY — cannot modify code. ([AAnkacHH/ankach-dev-framework](https://github.com/AAnkacHH/ankach-dev-framework/blob/HEAD/workflows/dev-pipeline/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that checks a change against memoirist's README promises, specs (API_CONTRACT, DATABASE_SCHEMA, DECISIONS, USER_FLOWS) and rules (security, api, backend, frontend). Use after finishing a sprint task or before committing, e.g. "have spec-reviewer check this task". ([aarrroon/memoirist](https://github.com/aarrroon/memoirist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Verifies every AC in requirement.md is actually implemented in code, not just claimed in test counts. Reads requirement.md, source files, and code-review.md (optional). For each AC, locates the implementation (search for AC ID in source, test names, controller annotations) and verifies the code does what the AC requires. Returns spec-review.md with per-AC LOCATED / LOCATED_BUT_INCORRECT / MISSING / DEFERRED. Runs after tdd-green-impl, before code-reviewer (Superpowers spec-before-quality pattern). ([abd-abdur/sdd-harness](https://github.com/abd-abdur/sdd-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Stage 1 of the SDD two-stage review gate. Runs after every implementer run and BEFORE code-reviewer. Verifies the implementation matches the spec exactly — nothing more, nothing less — by reading actual code, not trusting the implementer's report. Does NOT evaluate code quality. Read-only. Adapted from obra/superpowers SDD. ([Abstract-Data/campaignfinance-2023](https://github.com/Abstract-Data/campaignfinance-2023/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: 收片用的「Spec 轴」只读审查:输入任务书(或契约)+ diff,只报三类——要求了没做 / 没要求却做了 / 做了但看起来不对,每条引任务书原句。不审 bug 不审风格(那是 /code-review 的轴),不改任何文件。 ([AbyssCN/claude-lead-harness](https://github.com/AbyssCN/claude-lead-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/spec-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **spec-reviewer**: Checks a diff against the spec it was meant to implement (the linked issue, the plan the user approved, or a spec file the caller names) and reports missing or partial requirements, unrequested behaviour, and requirements implemented wrongly, each quoting its spec line. The spec axis of /agent-core:review. Changes no file. ([adhibuchori/agent-config-kit](https://github.com/adhibuchori/agent-config-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-core/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Review an already-written feature spec under specs/ for ambiguities, uncovered edge cases, and contradictions with other specs or with docs/requirements.md. Use after a spec is drafted (or edited) and before /speckit-plan, or any time an existing spec is revisited. Complements, and does not replace, /speckit-clarify (ambiguity resolution on the current spec before planning) and /speckit-analyze (spec/plan/tasks consistency within one feature) — this agent's job is adversarial re-reading of a spec that already exists, and checking it against every other spec, not just itself. ([adrisons/gym-log-app](https://github.com/adrisons/gym-log-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Проверяет изменения ветки на соответствие ТЗ, CLAUDE.md, архитектуре и Definition of Done. Используй после реализации фичи и перед созданием PR. Не правит код, только отчёт. ([ahahaharu/puzzleup-site](https://github.com/ahahaharu/puzzleup-site/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a spec in specs/ before any code is written. Use when a new or changed spec is ready for implementation. ([AhmadSaleh22/footprint-to-ifc](https://github.com/AhmadSaleh22/footprint-to-ifc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Spec-compliance reviewer. It checks a diff against its task's written requirements (missing, extra or misread) without trusting the implementer's report. Outside the supervised option it runs before code-quality-reviewer. Read-only. Pins set by the owner; change only on their instruction. ([aidan-bailey/loom](https://github.com/aidan-bailey/loom/blob/HEAD/flow/plugin/agents/spec-reviewer.md))
- **spec-reviewer**: Review a feature's spec.md, plan.md, tasks.md or fix.md in specs/, or a requirements.md in docs/requirements/, against the constitution and higher-level documents. - **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a spec file (docs/specs/&lt;feat&gt;/spec.md) for ambiguity, missing acceptance criteria, hidden assumptions, and untestable claims. Acts as the clarify-gate before /feature-plan begins. - **spec-reviewer**: Requirements analyst that reviews docs/project_spec.md for completeness, clarity, and scope discipline. - **spec-reviewer**: Revisa una spec.md de NEXO SIS en busca de huecos, ambigüedades, requisitos no verificables, mezcla de qué/cómo y contradicciones con la constitución o con specs/000-decisiones.md. - **spec-reviewer**: Checks that a Person Service PR delivers exactly its slice from the develop-phase table and docs/plan.md, against the ~400-line Java target. Use on every PR. - **spec-reviewer**: Checks whether code changes fully implement the request or plan passed in the prompt, requirement by requirement, and flags scope beyond it. - **spec-reviewer**: Reviews a diff against the spec it was meant to implement. Invoke it immediately after any spec.md/plan.md/tasks.md is created or edited, and do not proceed to the next stage until it returns VERDICT: APPROVED. - **spec-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that checks the current changes against one task file's acceptance criteria (and its roadmap check, in roadmap mode), scope, CLAUDE.md hard rules, the decisions log, where its follow-ups were placed, its roadmap ticks, and its task list Done cells.
